| **USER-FINISHER-CARRY** — a string finisher lands its mob about one step outside the hero's reach (D6a) | **User direction 2026-10-11 (D6a):** "Walk-back after finishers. In Story, shorten the finisher's airborne carry on launched standard mobs. The mob should land about one step outside hit-1 reach, not several. The shared 4.5× finisher (user-locked) is untouched. Lincoln stays the longest carry. Measure before and after." **Addendum (user, 2026-10-11):** "Elites also get the shorter finisher carry (D6a)." Orchestrator default: Lincoln leads in final landing distance (spec D's heavy clause), and fast melee mobs landing one step out and walking back are accepted. *Definitions:* **land** = the mob's first floor contact after the finisher (a flyer: its stun's expiry), measured pivot to pivot (group C's `carry` / `land_gap` tick); **one step** = 0.5 u (30 px); **hit-1 reach** = the attacker's live grounded hit-1 far edge (`PlayerController.StoryStringSpanPixels(0)`, width % and the AttackRange minor included) + the mob's hurtbox half-width (Standard 23, Elite 29 px). Supersedes `S1-STUNNED-KNOCKBACK-FRICTION`'s single 600 px/s² air drag for finisher flights, and V7.4's pressure exit at stun expiry, which for a finisher flight runs at touchdown. | `EnemyController.ArmLaunch` (from `OnHurtboxHit`, after the stun step, only when the hit wrote knockback and left the mob stunned): a Basic `combo_3` hit authored to launch, on a **Standard or Elite** `EnemyController`, whose `SourceActorId` resolves to a `PlayerController`, arms a **finisher flight**: target = own hit-1 reach + 30 px, or for an authored finisher force (`BasicAttackKnockback` × the profile's finisher tenths / 10) ≥ 18 (only Lincoln's 22.5) at least the widest profile's hit-1 reach (template 96 px × `BasicComboRules.WidestStringReachWidthPercent` 120 % + the half-width) + 30 + 15 px; carry = max(0, target − the gap at the finisher); deceleration `v²/(2(carry + v/120))` from the launch speed the hit wrote (`StoryEnemyCombatRules.FinisherCarryDeceleration`), applied as `max(S1, it)` in `ApplyStunnedKnockbackFriction`, with S1's edge cancel after it. A ground mob still airborne when its stun expires keeps flying with its **AI suspended** (no chase step, commit, band, facing or blink; the getup armor still arms) and its pressure exit deferred to touchdown; a 120-frame post-stun cap backs it. The flight ends on touchdown, a stun expiry while grounded or flying, any knockback write (`TryApplyKnockback` clears it; the hit re-arms), an Armored Recovery trip, death, a grab or a throw, the rewind freeze and the pool and rewind resets. Not affected: bosses, Lincoln's launching hit 2, the Up-Attack and Down-Air, Specials, Ultimates, throws, and any payload with no resolvable hero. **Measured** (`StoryFinisherCarryTests`, real strings in real physics frames, Normal, full-HP aggroed `chrono_slasher` at pushbox contact; pivot-to-pivot landing and its offset from the hero's own hit-1 reach, **before → after**): Einstein 102.0 (−26.6) → 158.6 (+30.0); Joan 165.7 (+56.3) → 139.4 (+30.0); Leonardo 82.4 (−51.0) → 159.9 (+26.5); **Lincoln 361.8 (+257.2) → 183.5 (+78.9)**; Cleopatra 61.5 (−76.7) → 157.8 (+19.6, S1-limited); Tesla 82.4 (−36.6) → 147.0 (+28.0); Shakespeare 102.0 (−21.8) → 153.8 (+30.0); Mozart 71.8 (−52.0) → 153.9 (+30.1); Tubman 107.5 (−11.5) → 149.1 (+30.1). Before, every aggroed slasher steered back mid-air after its stun; after, none does. Touchdown 61–76 frames after the finisher connects, against an armor end of 67–69. Einstein tip-range string on a passive slasher 353.0 (+224.4) → 158.7 (+30.1); close string on a `laser_rifle_infantry` (ranged primary) 233.7 (+105.1) → 158.0 (+29.4). **Elites** (`chrono_guard_elite`, resistance zeroed): Joan 135.6 (+20.2) → 134.5 (+19.1, S1-limited); Lincoln 196.7 (+86.1, steered back) → 182.8 (+72.2, no steering). Lincoln lands farthest from a close string (184 px; next Leonardo 160), but his finisher-only displacement (81 px) is not the longest, because his launching hit 2 has already carried the mob to 102 px (spec D §7.1). Bot "before" (`bots_before.md`, pooled, `play`): re-approach after a finisher median / p90 80 / 166 frames on Normal, 84 / 156 on Easy; Standard finisher carry / land gap median 189 / 149 px (Normal), 217 / 114 px (Easy). **After** (2026-10-12, `absolum_research/flow/bots_after.md`, every combat-flow group in the tree, seeds 0–3 pooled, the landing measured from where the hero stood at the finisher): Normal standards land 256 → 116 px from Joan (her target 116) and 269 → 131 px from Mozart (target 131), elites alike (Joan 205 → 116, Mozart 198 → 130), with 81–90 % of landings within ±30 px of the target (2–13 % before); the Standard finisher carry median is 189 → 53 px (`play`); the re-approach median / p90 is 80 / 166 → 55 / 123 frames on Normal `play` (batch ranges 64–93 → 54–58) and 84 / 156 → 82 / 146 on Easy `play` (within the batch spread). **Side effect:** the bots' next swing after a finisher connects less often (Normal `play` 94.6 → 87.8 %, `confirm` policy 93.5 → 79.7 %), because they re-swing about 47 frames after the connect while the mob touches down 60–75 frames after it, one step out; those whiffs are grounded hit 1s at a median gap of 30–31 px. A player who swings on rhythm may feel the same (spec D §7.2–7.3). **Amended 2026-10-12 (gameplay update, `USER-STORY-STATIC-KNOCKBACK`):** Story knockback is static, so the launch the finisher writes no longer grows with the mob's missing HP (on these fresh slashers it fell by ×1.43–1.78: Einstein 686 → 450 px/s, Lincoln 1331 → 750, Cleopatra 428 → 300), and the flight now decelerates at the carry deceleration **alone**, in place of S1's 1800 / 600 px/s² for the whole flight, not at `max(S1, it)`: with static knockback and the old `max`, Cleopatra's finisher landed 104.1 px, 34 px inside her own 138.2 px reach. **Re-measured** (`StoryFinisherCarryTests`, Normal, a full-HP aggroed `chrono_slasher` at pushbox contact; the landing and its offset from the hero's own hit-1 reach, 2026-10-11 → 2026-10-12): Einstein 158.6 (+30.0) → 158.6 (+30.0); Joan 139.4 (+30.0) → 139.4 (+30.0); Leonardo 159.9 (+26.5) → 163.4 (+30.0); **Lincoln 183.5 (+78.9) → 183.3 (+78.7)**; Cleopatra 157.8 (+19.6) → 159.6 (+21.4, airtime-limited); Tesla 147.0 (+28.0) → 149.1 (+30.1); Shakespeare 153.8 (+30.0) → 153.8 (+30.0); Mozart 153.9 (+30.1) → 153.9 (+30.1); Tubman 149.1 (+30.1) → 149.0 (+30.0). Every hero lands 21.4–78.7 px outside its own hit-1 reach, Lincoln still farthest (next Leonardo, 163.4), and no mob steers back. Touchdown moved from 61–76 to **45–53 frames** after the connect (stun end 31–33 and the getup-armor end 67–69 unchanged); hits 1 and 2 are static too, so the gap at the finisher fell (Lincoln's launching hit 2 now leaves the mob at 74 px, not 102). Elites (`chrono_guard_elite`): Joan 134.5 (+19.1) → 137.1 (+21.7), Lincoln 182.8 (+72.2) → 180.1 (+69.5); the tip-range string 158.7 (+30.1) unchanged; `laser_rifle_infantry` 158.0 (+29.4) → 158.6 (+30.0). The ±6 px landing pin is now gated on `CarryStoppedInAir` (`PROVISIONAL-STATIC-KNOCKBACK-RULES` (5)). **Also amended 2026-10-12 (`USER-ENEMY-TURN-TAKING`):** a carried mob keeps its engagement slot until touchdown and hands it off there only to an eligible waiter. The bots' after-finisher whiff (they re-swing about 47 frames after the connect, while the mob touched down 60–75 frames after it) may shrink with touchdown at 45–53 frames; not re-measured, and bot batches from before and after 2026-10-12 are not comparable on carry, landing gap or re-approach frames. | User | A hands-on pass confirms "about one step" on close and tip-range strings; group C's Easy and Normal `play` re-approach medians are re-measured after (done 2026-10-12, above); a ruling on which "carry" Lincoln must lead (landing distance, implemented, or finisher-only displacement). |
| **USER-MOB-MELEE-RANGE** — melee mobs commit and hold inside their own reach (D6c) | **User direction 2026-10-11 (D6c):** "Mobs stay in range. Cap a melee mob's commit and stand-off distance at its own melee hitbox reach minus a margin, so it stops swinging at air. Applies to MeleeStrike and ChargeDash primaries only. Ranged and AreaPulse attacks are unchanged." | `EnemyController`: when the attack the mob will throw next (`PreviewNextAttack()`, a side-effect-free preview of `SelectNextAttack`, both now reading one `ResolveNextAttack`) is its own MeleeStrike / ChargeDash primary (authored, or the legacy synthesized 40 px melee), it **commits** only when the target is horizontally within `C = min(AttackRangePixels, pivot reach − 10)` (pivot reach = `\|HitboxOffset.X\| + HitboxSize.X/2` + a ChargeDash's travel + the hero's 20 px hurtbox half-width) **and** its box's height band overlaps the hero's 64 px hurtbox; while it **can** commit it never holds (walks in to C); while it **cannot** (cooldown running, or the hero above or below its box) it holds in a band on the horizontal gap engaged at `max(0.85 C, own pushbox half + 18 + 4 + 4.5 v/60)` and released at that × 1.10 / 0.85, so it rests outside pushbox contact and inside its reach. The V7.4 pressure exit and the Armored Recovery answer use the same test: out of reach, the pressure exit falls through to Chase and the armored answer advances armored in Chase and swings the moment the target is in reach, still inside its armored window, ignoring the primary's cooldown and with no reaction delay, as the in-reach answer does (with no target it still commits as before). Every other attack (projectile primaries, AreaPulse, every elite or secondary ability), the engage blink, the reactive Phase Skip and `BossController` keep `AttackRangePixels`. Roster (spec D §1.3 table): the 16 melee primaries now commit at 60–77 px (e.g. `chrono_slasher` 90 → 60, `unbound_eraser` 180 → 77) and rest at 41–52 px. Bot "before" (`bots_before.md`, Normal): mob melee whiffs 64.1 % (`play`), 98 % of them out of reach; **after** (2026-10-12, `bots_after.md`, pooled): 11.7 % (`play`), 18.0 % (`basics`), 25.0 % (`spam`), Easy `play` 30.2 %; near misses at activation (a 1–40 px gap, the stand-off signature) per 100 swings: `chrono_slasher` 39.0 → 2.5, `lance_jab` 19.8 → 8.3, `cyber_cavalry_commander` 28.6 → 4.0; the whiffs left are mostly 41 px or more (the hero left during the wind-up); Normal mob melee swings fell 9.76 → 5.68 per run while their connects rose 3.50 → 4.69, the pressure rise spec D predicted. Limitations (recorded): the commit is measured against the hero's 40 × 64 hurtbox, so against Cleopatra's 36 px sand decoy it is 2 px generous, inside the margin; a mob does not commit at an airborne hero wholly above its box; a flying mob's diagonal approach can overshoot its band by up to √2×, with the pushbox as the net; nine elites still run the legacy 40 × 40 placeholder primary. **Amended 2026-10-12 (gameplay update, `USER-ENEMY-TURN-TAKING`):** a melee mob that holds no engagement slot holds on the director's wait ring (rank 0 at 200 / 170 / 150 px on Easy / Normal / Hard, +64 px per rank) instead of walking in to C, and walks in only once granted a slot; an owed Armored Recovery answer bypasses the ring, and the pressure exit is never gated, so a struck waiter still answers in reach. A ranged stand-off band is capped at `StoryEngagementRules.RangedHoldMaxPixels` 560 (engage) and `RangedHoldReleaseMaxPixels` 600 (release), which changes only `infrared_border_sentry` (688.5 → 560, release 891 → 600). **Amended 2026-10-13 (FIX-ENEMY):** inside an engaged encounter lock a melee member whose target stands more than 64 px below steps off a ledge toward it and never holds its band over the hero (`PROVISIONAL-LOCK-MEMBER-REACH` (4)). | User | Group C3's mob-swing telemetry shows the stand-off whiffs gone (an "after" batch; met at bot level 2026-10-12, above); a hands-on pass. |
| **USER-STORY-WALL-BOUNCE** — a launched or thrown mob bounces off a wall (D9) | **User direction 2026-10-11 (D9):** "Trigger: a mob launched into an Environment wall faster than ~300 px/s horizontally. Effect: it bounces back at 40% of that speed; it takes 10% of the launching hit's damage as impact damage, once per launch. Scope: Story only, never Fighter." **Addendum (user, 2026-10-11):** "Thrown standard mobs wall-bounce (D9), just like launched ones." | `EnemyController`: a player-dealt (`IsPlayerDealt`), launching hit that writes knockback on a non-phasing, still-stunned Standard or Elite arms one bounce (`ArmLaunch`), its impact `ScaleIntakeDamage(intake, 0.1)` of the hit's pre-defense Story intake (half away from zero, at least 1); an Ultimate launch's impact also ignores the bubble. `LaunchThrown(velocity, bowling, throwDamage)` arms the throw's own bounce, its impact a tenth of the throw's damage (`PlayerController.ResolveStoryThrow` now passes it). After each `MoveAndSlide` (the stunned / finisher-flight step, and `ProcessThrownFlight`), a latched mob with `IsOnWall()`, a pre-move horizontal speed **> 300 px/s**, a facing wall normal (`normal.x × sign(v) ≤ −0.7`) **and** the existing body-centre wall ray (`HasWallAhead`, so a floor seam or step lip never counts) is set to `−0.4 ×` its pre-move speed (vertical kept, the current deceleration kept) and takes the impact once through the private `TakeDamage` — defenses once there (RadiantBurn, the bubble), frontal reduction never (no origin), no stun, knockback, hitstop, poise, meter, Rally, landed-hit report or Punish multiplier; a lethal impact dies normally. It floats an untinted number and shakes like a non-launching direct hit. The latch clears with the stun's natural expiry (a finisher flight keeps it to touchdown; a throw keeps it to its landing), any knockback write, the bounce itself, and every reset the D6a flight has. Never: the Fighter sim, `BossController`, the Mirror clone, the hero, a phaser, a non-launching shove, a mob whose stun has ended outside a finisher flight. Impact sizes: a string finisher deals 1 HP on a standard or elite (Lincoln 2 on a standard), a Special launch more; spec D §1.4 tabulates the bounce radii (after D6a the slasher's is 52–81 px from a string finisher). **Amended 2026-10-12 (gameplay update, `USER-STORY-STATIC-KNOCKBACK`):** with static Story knockback, **whether a hit bounces depends on the hero and the mob, never on the mob's HP** (before, a hurt mob could always be bounced). A string finisher bounces a mob of weight `w` only when `F × 60 / (1 + w) > 300` (F = `BasicAttackKnockback` × the finisher tenths / 10): Cleopatra (F 9.0) bounces only `holo_page`, `hologram_drone`, `voltaic_shock_drone` (0.7) and `laser_archer` (0.75), never a `chrono_slasher` (exactly 300 px/s; the floor is strict); Leonardo and Tesla (11.25) every standard and the 1.0 / 1.2 elites (`unbound_eraser`, `chrono_warden`); Einstein, Shakespeare and Tubman (13.5) everything below weight 1.7; Mozart (13.75) everything below 1.75; Joan (15.75) and Lincoln (22.5) everything. **Re-measured string-finisher bounce radii on `chrono_slasher`** (how far a wall face may stand from the leading edge and still bounce the mob; `StoryFinisherCarryTests`, Normal, 2026-10-11 → 2026-10-12): Cleopatra 52.5 → **0**, Tesla 68.9 → 33.7, Leonardo 76.2 → 39.3, Joan 64.3 → 54.7, Tubman 74.3 → 55.3, Shakespeare 79.8 → 55.9, Einstein 81.3 → 61.4, Mozart 79.4 → 62.2, Lincoln 77.0 → 92.7; on `chrono_guard_elite` Joan 21.4 → 15.9 and Lincoln 82.1 → 78.2. **Forward throws** (a real grab and throw on a full-HP slasher; the thrown flight has no drag, so its bounce radius is the whole flight): vx Einstein and Shakespeare 337.5 → 300 and Tubman 345 → 300 px/s (they no longer bounce it), Joan 402.5 → 350 and Lincoln 600 → 500 (still bounce), Leonardo, Tesla, Mozart and Cleopatra never; a throw now bounces a standard of weight `w` only when `180 × baseKB / (1 + w) > 300` (Lincoln every standard; Joan every standard but `shock_shield_legionnaire`; Einstein, Shakespeare and Tubman only `holo_page`, `laser_archer` and the two 0.7 drones). Every encounter-lock barrier is an Environment wall, so the bounce also fires in lock fights. | User | A hands-on pass near walls; a ruling on the open items in spec D §7 (impact size, bounce radius). |
| **PROVISIONAL-SPACING-WALLS-RULES** — the details D6 and D9 left open | D6b gives the far edge, front-only, the kept vertical coverage and the table's home only. D6a gives "about one step outside hit-1 reach", the untouched 4.5× finisher, "Lincoln stays the longest carry" and (addendum) elites too; D6c gives "cap a melee mob's commit and stand-off distance at its own melee hitbox reach minus a margin", MeleeStrike / ChargeDash primaries only; D9 gives "~300 px/s", 40 %, 10 % of the launching hit's damage once per launch, Story only, never Fighter, and (addendum) thrown standards too. | *Provisional, agent-chosen (spec D §1.2 and §6).* **(2) D6b:** (a) the aerial **near** edge is the grounded near edge (0.4 / 0.3 / 0.3 u × width % × the AttackRange minor, as the reach-geometry step scales the grounded box), not a near edge pinned at the pivot; (b) the facing is read at active start, so a hero who turns during the aerial startup strikes in front of its new facing (the swing-start latch would otherwise strike behind, breaking front-only); (c) the vertical span keeps the pre-D6b Story pixels exactly, and the minor scales the aerial height about its centre because it scales the grounded height (`PROVISIONAL-REACH-MINOR-SHAPE` (a)); (d) the span is stored in world units as the authored pixels over 60, with the centre measured **Y down from the feet** (Story's convention), unlike the grounded table's Y-up offset from the hurtbox centre, because nothing but Story reads it; (e) `PlayerController.StoryStringSpanPixels(step)` is a read-only seam returning a hero's live (near, far) string span in pixels, for sub-pass (ii). **(1) D6a** (sub-pass (ii), 2026-10-11): (a) the landing target is the attacker's live hit-1 pivot reach + 30 px; (b) the heavy-finisher clause — an authored finisher force ≥ 18 lands the mob at least the widest profile's hit-1 reach + 30 + 15 px — is "Lincoln stays the longest carry", read as the farthest landing from the same gap; (c) the carry is realised as the deceleration `max(S1, v²/(2(D + v/120)))`, which never lengthens a carry S1 already ends sooner (so Cleopatra's light finisher lands at +20, and grounded-first-frame finishers a few px short of target); (d) Standard and, by the user's addendum, Elite mobs, flying standards included (their carry ends at stun expiry); (e) a ground mob's flight, carry and bounce latch outlive the stun until touchdown, with the AI and the pressure exit suspended, behind a 120-frame post-stun cap; (f) no resolvable hero attacker (`SourceActorId` not a `PlayerController`) → S1; Lincoln's launching hit 2, the directional strikes, Specials, Ultimates and throws keep S1. (g) **Orchestrator defaults (`DECISIONS.md` addendum D):** Lincoln leads by final landing distance ((b)), and a fast melee mob that lands one step out and walks straight back is accepted. **(3) D6c:** (a) margin 10 px, measured against the Story hero hurtbox (40 × 64, `StoryCombatRules.HeroHurtbox*Pixels`); (b) the commit test is the horizontal gap ≤ C with the vertical-overlap gate (touching edges do not overlap); the decoy's 36 px hurtbox is measured as the hero's; (c) the stand-off engage is `max(0.85 C, contact + 4 + 4.5 v/60)` with v the unscaled chase speed, released at × 1.10 / 0.85; (d) no hold while the mob can commit; (e) only when the previewed next attack is the melee primary, so an elite whose signature ability is next commits from its authored range and an elite cooldown coming up during the reaction delay after a capped commit fires from the capped distance; (f) a ChargeDash primary's reach adds its dash travel; (g) the engage blink and the elite abilities keep `AttackRange`; (h) an out-of-reach Armored Recovery answer advances armored in Chase with the answer owed (`EnemyController.ArmoredAnswerOwed`) and commits it the moment the target is in reach under the in-reach answer's rules: no cooldown gate, so a trip landing while the primary's cooldown runs still walks in instead of holding at its band, and no reaction delay, so the telegraph opens inside the armored window (2026-10-11 review fix; the first cut routed it through the ordinary commit, which held on a running cooldown and rolled a 30–45-frame reaction delay that outlasted the 0.6 s standard window); the debt lapses with the window, which is not extended, and the ordinary pressure exit and cooldown then apply; death, a stun, a grab, losing the target, the rewind freeze and the pool and rewind resets clear it; with no target it commits as before. **(4) D9:** (a) armed by any player-dealt launch that writes knockback on a stunned, non-phasing `EnemyController`, and (addendum) by every throw of a non-phasing mob; (b) a strict 300 px/s pre-move horizontal speed, a facing-normal test (`\|nx\| ≥ 0.7`) and the body-centre ray confirm; (c) one bounce and one impact per launch or throw; (d) the bounce keeps the vertical speed and the current deceleration; (e) impact = 10 % of the pre-defense intake (for a throw, the throw's damage), rounded half away from zero, minimum 1, then defenses once (an Ultimate launch ignores the bubble); (f) no intake scale, stun, hitstop, poise, meter, Rally or Punish effect; the F9 number (untinted) and the non-launching shake; (g) phasers, bosses, the hero and the Mirror clone excluded. Test seams: `EnemyController.IsInFinisherFlight`, `FinisherCarryDeceleration`, `LastFinisherLandingTargetPixels`, `IsWallBounceArmed`, `LastWallImpactDealt`, `LastWallBouncePreMoveVelocityX`, `PreviewNextAttack()`, `CommitRangePixels`, `PrimaryCommitRangePixels`, `StandOffEngagePixels`, `StandOffResumePixels`, `ArmoredAnswerOwed` (and the internal `SetAttackCooldownForTest`). **Amended 2026-10-12 (gameplay update):** item (1)(c) — the carry is now realised as the deceleration `v²/(2(D + v/120))` **alone**, in place of S1 for the whole flight (`USER-STORY-STATIC-KNOCKBACK`, `PROVISIONAL-STATIC-KNOCKBACK-RULES` (2)), so a carry that runs out in the air stops on the target by construction and one that touches down first lands short: Cleopatra now lands +21.4 (airtime-limited), the other close-string slashers +30.0 ± 0.1, Lincoln +78.7. Item (1)(e) — a carried mob also keeps its engagement slot until touchdown (`USER-ENEMY-TURN-TAKING`). Item (2), D6b — the aerial string is retired by `USER-AERIAL-SINGLE-STRIKE`: (a), (b) and (c) now describe the single air strike's box (string hit 1's x-span and near edge, the facing read at active start, the retired aerial hit 3's vertical span scaled about its centre by the minor), (d)'s per-hit table is replaced by `BasicComboRules.AirAttackBoxCentreYUnits` / `AirAttackBoxHeightUnits` (still Y down from the feet), and (e) `StoryStringSpanPixels` is unchanged. Item (3), D6c — a melee waiter holds on the engagement director's ring until it is granted a slot. | Unassigned | A ruling on each, or a playtest that leaves them alone. `StoryAerialStringReachTests`, `StoryFinisherCarryTests`, `StoryWallBounceTests`, the D6c cases in `EnemyControllerTests` / `EnemyRosterContentTests` and `StoryEnemyCombatRulesTests` pin today's rules. |

## Confirmed open deviations — level fixes and input (gamepad Time Freeze on L3, dust never expires, a Restoration Font in every level), 2026-10-11

User-directed Story input rule from `DECISIONS.md` D11, built on `main`'s working tree on 2026-10-11 (uncommitted on `d2e52e0`; committed as `2e9ca79` on 2026-10-12) by spec E's Pass A, on top of the gamepad-defaults step (`VERIFY-GAMEPAD-ULTIMATE-CHORD` (b), `PROVISIONAL-GAMEPAD-OVERRIDE-NO-MIGRATION`). It is **Story-only**: Time Freeze has no `GameplayButtons` bit, so nothing under `scripts/FighterSim/` was touched and no component, snapshot field, input-frame bit, protocol, save field, schema version or translation key changed; no Fighter hash moved. One English value changed (`tutorial_step_time_freeze`). Spec E's later passes (D12, mob dust never expires; D13, a Restoration Font in every level) append their rows here. **Pass B (D12, 2026-10-11)** added the four dust rows after `USER-GAMEPAD-TIME-FREEZE-L3`. It is Story-only too: `ChronalDustPickup`, `LevelManager`, `StoryDropSystem`, `StoryManager` (`LastLevelDustLost`), `LevelRewardDirectory` (the duplicate `LastTierBonusDust` deleted) and the two results call sites (`StoryLevelControllerBase.PresentCompletion`, `Level01Controller`); no save field, schema version, translation key, component or Fighter hash changed. Tests: +5 `[TestCase]` (`StoryDropsAndRewindTests` +2, `StoryLevelControllerBaseTests` +2, `SummonRewardProvenanceTests` +1), 3001 → 3006, all five failing against the pre-D12 behaviour. **The D12 review fix-up (2026-10-11)** re-fixed the results' losses half (a same-attempt reload zeroed the in-memory loss tally, the exit fee was never recorded, and the dust held across a reload was on no line) and added `PROVISIONAL-RESULTS-CARRIED-DUST` after the fixed row: `StoryManager` (`RecordAttemptDustLoss`, `LevelDustCarriedIn` / `LastLevelDustCarriedIn`, the carry in `RestoreAttemptStateFromSave`, the exit fee in `CommitOrderlyExit`), `LevelRewardDirectory.ClearDustLoss`, `LevelResultsPanel` (the `DustCarried` line and a ten-argument `ShowResults`) and both results call sites. One translation key was added (`results_dust_carried`) and one English value changed (`results_dust_losses`); no save field, schema version, component or Fighter hash changed. Tests: +3 `[TestCase]` (`StoryLevelControllerBaseTests` +1, `CompletionOnlyBankingTests` +2), 3006 → 3009, all three failing against the step's code; `CompletionBanksLiveDustPickupsFirstAndNothingPaysAfterIt` now pins the campaign level first, so its post-completion kill resolves against Chicago's ledger and pins the kill guard whatever test ran before it (no count change). **Pass C (D13, 2026-10-11)** added the four Restoration Font rows after `PROVISIONAL-RESULTS-CARRIED-DUST`. It is Story-only too: `RestorationFont` (the `Create` factory, the `restoration_font` group, the `RefusesWhile` predicate, the `ChannelRadial`, the F10 commit/credit/cancel calls and the `EventBus.OnPlayerDied` death hook), `InteractionArea.RefreshPrompt` (now public), `StoryManager` (the font commit writes the wallet; a Timeline Collapse clears a pending heal), `StoryLevelControllerBase` (the `RestorationFontPosition` hook), the fourteen Level 2–15 controllers, `Level01Controller`, `Level00Controller` (the font built at the Combat Trial, plus the named checkpoint and wall constants and the `BeginCombatTrialForTesting` seam) and `PlaytestRunner.RouteInteract`. One English value changed (`font_channel_prompt`); no translation key, save field, schema version, component or Fighter hash changed. Tests: +11 `[TestCase]` (`StoryHealingLoopTests` +7, `SecretCachePlacementTests` +1, `Level01ContentTests` +1, `Level00FlowSceneTests` +1, `Level02ContentTests` +1), 3009 → 3020; the two existing font cases in `StoryHealingLoopTests` gained slot −1 and pending-heal hygiene in place (no count change). **The D13 review fix-up (2026-10-12)** moved three fonts out of a hazard's reach (Florence, Levels 7 and 14), made the hazard clearance a tested rule (the shared non-suite test helper `FontHazardClearance`) and added the attempt-level death cancel in `ChronalRewindManager.OnPlayerDied`: +1 `[TestCase]` (`StoryHealingLoopTests.ADeathCancelsAPendingHealThatNoFontIsTracking`), 3020 → 3021, with four existing font cases tightened in place. The user row `USER-RESTORATION-FONT-EVERY-LEVEL` was added at the finalize step so that D13 has its own user row; the agent-chosen half stays in `PROVISIONAL-FONT-PLACEMENT`.

| ID / area | Intended behavior (decision) | Observed build value (revision) | Owner | Acceptance criteria |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **USER-GAMEPAD-TIME-FREEZE-L3** — the gamepad Time Freeze default is L3 (D11) | **User direction 2026-10-11 (D11):** "Gamepad Time Freeze moves to L3 (`JoyButton.LeftStick` = 7). The bug batch has already moved Movement to LB (9), Special 2 to RB (10), and the Ultimate chord to LB+RB." **Supersedes:** `design-godot.md` :405, the Level 0 calibration lesson ("`gameplay_time_freeze` (**R / Back / Select**)"); :2110, Time Freeze's input line ("**R** / **Back or Select**"); :2290, the Controls table ("`TimeFreeze` … `Back` / `Select`"); and `COMFORT_SETTINGS.md` C01c :104 ("R / Back or Select"). | `project.godot` `gameplay_time_freeze`: the joypad event is `button_index` 7 (was 4, Back, inherited from the retired rewind); the keyboard default, physical keycode 82 (`R`), is unchanged, and nothing else in the gamepad layout moved. Button 7 was bound to nothing, so L3 collides with no authored action; Back (4) and Guide (5) now carry no authored action. The runtime path is unchanged: `TimeFreezeController` polls `Input.IsActionJustPressed(InputManager.Actions.TimeFreeze)` on every device, and a human L3 press freezes the Level 13 Mirror clone as Back did (its input source cannot press Time Freeze). Pinned by `GamepadDefaultBindingTests.TimeFreezeDefaultsToTheLeftStickClickAlone` (the enum value 7, the authored joypad half exactly `[7]`, keyboard `R` kept; fails on the old `[4]`); `GamepadOverrideRepairTests` was rewritten in place for the new defaults (no count change). **No migration** (`PROVISIONAL-GAMEPAD-OVERRIDE-NO-MIGRATION`): a profile with no Time Freeze row inherits L3 at once; a saved Time Freeze row, even from a keyboard-only rebind, keeps what it carried (Back works). The Controls tab labels the bind "Pad Button 7" through `InputBindingService.Describe` (there is no glyph table, a pre-existing C01c gap), and "Reset to default" reads `project.godot`, so it restores L3. The Level 0 objective `tutorial_step_time_freeze` now reads "Press Time Freeze to stop time, then slip past and reach the marker" (was "Press R …", wrong on a pad); it names the action, not a button, because `Describe` would render "Pad Button 7" and would show one device's binding to a player on the other. | User (design amendment) | The GDD master and the C01c contract amended at the four lines cited and re-synced, and one pad session confirming that L3 does not fire by accident during ordinary or hard stick movement (a stray Time Freeze spends its 45 s cooldown). |
| **USER-DUST-NEVER-EXPIRES** — a mob dust pickup is collected at its timer (D12) | **User direction 2026-10-11 (D12):** "A mob dust pickup that reaches its 10-second expiry is auto-collected into the wallet instead of vanishing. Its claim is committed, as for a normal collection. The authored 720 / 1,000 totals become reachable." **Supersedes** `design-godot.md` :891 (the pool table's "10s idle expiration" for Chronal Dust). **Narrows** `DUST_ECONOMY.md` :12 ("Missed pickups … can reduce income"): a mob pickup can no longer be missed by waiting. | `ChronalDustPickup._PhysicsProcess` calls `Collect()` when a pickup with `NeverExpires == false` (every kill drop, `Source == Mob`) reaches `AutoCollectSeconds` (10 s; renamed from `ExpirationTime`, value unchanged). Collection is unchanged: `LevelRewardDirectory.CommitClaim(SourceID)`, `RaiseChronalDustCollected` (wallet and the level's tally), `RaiseDustAwardCollected { Source = Mob }`, the pickup cue and the HUD `+N` float, then the pool. The 150 px magnet and its speed are unchanged. Boss, Extractor and Secret pickups never had a timer. Pinned by `StoryDropsAndRewindTests.AMobPickupThatReachesItsTimerIsCollectedIntoTheWalletWithItsClaimCommitted` (one award of 4, Mob attribution, the claim committed, idempotent afterwards; the old code returned it to the pool unpaid). | User (design amendment) | The master and `DUST_ECONOMY.md` amended at :891 and :12, and a thorough-route playthrough (or a bot batch) showing no mob source left Issued at completion. |
| **PROVISIONAL-DUST-SETTLE-RULES** — the details D12 left open | D12 names the timer collection and the committed claim; nothing else. The 2026-10-11 addendum's orchestrator default (spec E question 1): "Boss, Extractor and Secret pickups still on the ground at the seal are paid automatically." | *Provisional, agent-chosen (spec E §1 D12, revision 3, with the addendum applied):* (1) **Timer and magnet.** The timer, the magnet and the 150 px radius are unchanged. The timer pauses wherever the pickup is frozen (Time Freeze, the death rewind including its pre-hold and playback, the R03 hold, the collapse beat; `story_loot` takes every suspension latch) and under tree pause, and resumes with its remaining time (`StoryDropsAndRewindTests.AMobPickupsTimerPausesUnderTimeFreezeAndResumesWhereItStopped`: 300 live, 900 frozen and 290 live ticks pay nothing, then it pays within 40). A dead hero whose world is not yet suspended may still auto-collect into the in-memory wallet (harmless: Smothered persists before presentation, Restart clears the wallet, Quit reloads from save). (2) **Completion settles every pickup on the ground.** `LevelManager.CompleteLevel` (the one chokepoint the base controller, Florence and the Tutorial all reach) calls `ChronalDustPickup.SettleOutstandingDust(level root)` **before** it sets the new scene-local `LevelManager.IsComplete` and raises `OnLevelComplete`: every live pickup under the level (in the tree, not pool-parked, not already collected) is `Collect()`ed, **mob drops and, per the addendum, boss, Extractor and Secret awards alike**. Their dust therefore counts toward the tier bonus, the deposit and the results lines. **This amends `DUST_ECONOMY.md` :76** ("commits actually collected/retained rewards … Boss defeat still spawns its existing physical pickup and does not auto-credit or deposit it"): the seal now collects whatever is still on the ground. (3) **Nothing pays or issues after completion.** `ChronalDustPickup.Collect()` is idempotent (a `_collected` latch, cleared only by `OnSpawn`) and releases a pickup **unpaid** once `LevelManager.IsLevelCompleteFor(pickup)` (a complete LevelManager in group `level_manager` whose parent, never the tree root, is an ancestor of the node); `StoryDropSystem.SpawnKillDust` issues nothing once its level is complete. Why: `OnLevelComplete` deposits and zeroes the wallet and then re-keys the reward directory (to the next level, or a reset copy of Level 15's own), and a fresh entry does not clear the wallet, so before this a pickup touched or a mob killed under the results overlay paid into the next level's bank or claimed the next level's first source of its type. A kill landed in the same physics flush as the completion issues nothing (only Level 0 can complete inside a flush, and its ledger pays nothing); a pickup spawned after the seal vanishes unpaid, with no cue. No LevelManager (the Test Arena, unit hosts) reads "not complete", so those contexts behave as before. (4) **Pickup hygiene.** `_lifetime` is field-initialized to 10 s and reset with `_timeFrozen` on despawn; `IsCollected` is a public test seam and the settle filter. Pinned by `StoryLevelControllerBaseTests.CompletionBanksLiveDustPickupsFirstAndNothingPaysAfterIt` (a mob drop and an Extractor award both paid before `OnLevelComplete`; a Secret pickup collected afterwards pays nothing; a later kill spawns nothing) and `SummonRewardProvenanceTests.AKillAfterItsLevelCompletedIssuesNoRewardFromTheNextLevelsLedger` (the Chicago ledger's first source is still the next one issued after a post-completion kill). | Unassigned | The user confirms the seal settle of milestone pickups (then the master and `DUST_ECONOMY.md` :76 say so) or rules that the seal must collect nothing, and a playtest that reads a vanishing post-seal pickup as acceptable (or a ruling to settle it too). |
| **VERIFY-PENDING-MOB-PICKUP-RECOVERY** — mob pickups out at a Collapse, Snap, exit or crash are dropped (pre-existing; recorded with D12) | `DUST_ECONOMY.md` :73 ("Restore an uncollected source/pickup consistently without creating two copies"); `design-godot.md` :2122 and `STORY_PERSISTENCE.md` :31 / :108 ("Restore claimed and pending pickups"). The 2026-10-11 addendum's orchestrator default: "Up to 10 s of mob dust lost on a Collapse, exit or crash is accepted and recorded." | A Timeline Collapse (Acts I–II), an Anchor Snap (Act III), a voluntary exit, a crash or a scene teardown releases outstanding **mob** pickups unpaid (`ReleasePooledContent` / `OnDespawn`). Their sources were only Issued in memory, never saved, so `RestoreClaims` makes them re-issuable, and they pay again only if that encounter is fought again, which forward-only rooms often prevent. The F10 API for preserving them exists (`StoryManager.TryIssueReward` / `IsRewardPendingPickup`) but the kill-drop path has never used it. Restart Level is conformant (it clears everything). D12 shrinks the exposure to at most 10 s of kills (a mob pickup now collects itself at its timer) and leaves the rule itself untouched; that ≤ 10 s exposure is accepted provisionally by the orchestrator's default. Of the milestone pickups, a boss pickup has its own restore (`SealReadiness.RestorePendingBossPickup`) and so does a Secret (`SecretCache.RestorePendingAward`); an uncollected Extractor pickup has none (`ChronalExtractor.RestoreDestroyedState` rebuilds the machine broken and pays nothing), an adjacent pre-existing gap this row also covers. | Unassigned | Kill drops (and Extractor awards) issued through the attempt's reward-source record and restored once on resume, or a user ruling that the exposure stands. |
| ~~**RESULTS-DUST-LINES-AFTER-RESET**~~ — the results panel's tier-bonus, losses and carried dust (fixed 2026-10-11, with D12; the losses half re-fixed by the D12 review fix-up the same day) | F05's six-category itemisation adds the tier bonus and subtracts the losses, and its total equals what the level banked. | **Fixed 2026-10-11.** Before, three faults. (a) `StoryLevelControllerBase.PresentCompletion` and Florence's results call read `LevelRewardDirectory.LastTierBonusDust` / `DustLostThisAttempt` **after** `OnLevelComplete` → `AdvanceToNextLevel` → `ClearLevelAttemptState` → `ResetAttempt()` had zeroed both, so both lines read 0 on every level. (b) The loss tally is in memory only, and every fee is followed by a reload of the attempt before the level can complete (the Collapse's hub-portal resume `RestartCollapsedLevel(true)` → `LoadCurrentLevel` → `BeginLevelRun(resume)`, the Anchor Snap's in-place `_forceResumeNextRun` reload, a slot load after Quit to Main Menu); that reload's `RestoreAttemptStateFromSave` → `ClearLevelAttemptState` → `ResetAttempt()` zeroed the tally, so even after (a) was fixed the losses line read 0 in every slotted run, and the voluntary exit fee was never recorded at all. (c) The per-source category lines are the controllers' scene-local tallies, so the dust the attempt held across that reload appeared on no line, and the panel subtracted a fee from dust it never counted (earn 100, Collapse fee 20, earn 50 more, bonus 13: the level banked 143, the panel showed 50 + 13 − 20 = 43). Now: (a) `StoryManager.CompleteLevelRun` freezes `LastLevelDustLost` and the new `LastLevelDustCarriedIn` beside the other "last level" figures (first in `OnLevelComplete`, before the reset; like them they keep their old value when the run timer was not running), and both panels read them and the existing `LastLevelTierBonusDust` (Florence's bonus stays 0: it is untimed); the duplicate `LevelRewardDirectory.LastTierBonusDust` is deleted. (b) `StoryManager` keys the tally to the paying attempt's GUID (`RecordAttemptDustLoss`, used by `ApplyTimelineCollapseDustPenalty` and now also by `CommitOrderlyExit`, so the exit fee is a loss too), and `RestoreAttemptStateFromSave` carries it across its reset when the parked attempt is that attempt; any other attempt's reload starts from zero, and a fee charged to another attempt restarts the tally rather than adding to it (`LevelRewardDirectory.ClearDustLoss`). (c) `BeginLevelRun` captures `LevelDustCarriedIn` = the wallet at the load + the losses already taken from it, and the panel shows it on a new line (`results_dust_carried`, "Earned before resuming: {0}", hidden when 0) and adds it to the total, so categories + carried + bonus − losses equals the bank (agent choices: `PROVISIONAL-RESULTS-CARRIED-DUST`). Pinned by `StoryLevelControllerBaseTests.TheResultsPanelShowsTheTierBonusAndLossesTheCompletionApplied` (both labels through the real completion path; they read 0 before), `StoryLevelControllerBaseTests.AfterACollapseAndTheAnchorResumeTheResultsAddUpToWhatTheLevelBanked` (a slotted fresh entry, a real Collapse, the anchor resume and the real level's completion: losses 20, carried 100, mobs 50, the total label equal to the deposit), `CompletionOnlyBankingTests.AnAnchorSnapAndAnExitFeeBothReachTheResumedAttemptsResults` (an Act III Snap, a Quit to Main Menu fee and `ResumeCampaign`: losses 36, carried 100, the itemisation equal to the bank) and `CompletionOnlyBankingTests.AnotherAttemptNeverInheritsTheLossTally`; the three new cases fail on the code before the fix-up (the tally zeroed by the reload, the exit fee unrecorded, two attempts' fees summed). | Unassigned | Closed. The split the panel shows after a process restart is `PROVISIONAL-RESULTS-CARRIED-DUST` (4). |
| **PROVISIONAL-RESULTS-CARRIED-DUST** — how the results itemise dust held across a reload (D12 review fix-up) | `DUST_ECONOMY.md` :64: "The results screen itemizes required enemies, boss, Extractors, secret/other optional allocations, losses, and the tier bonus", and F05 wants the total to equal what the level banked. Act finales (5, 12, 15) author one each. **Use rules:** `CanInteract` refuses while the font's own restore runs (on Easy the second channel used to start 1.5 s into the 2 s restore and overwrite it, losing about a quarter of the first heal) and while the new `RefusesWhile` predicate says so — wired to `LevelComplete` (base), `_levelComplete` (Florence, Tutorial), because completion re-keys the use registry to the next level, so a spent font would read fresh and commit into the next attempt; a channel running when it turns true is interrupted with a refund, a paid restore completes; there is **no** boss-fight refusal (a pinned boss can still reach the Level 2 font through its 540 px band; damage interrupts and refunds). **F10 wiring:** the channel now commits through `StoryManager.CommitFontChannelCompletion` (use + pending heal together; it used to call the bare `RecordFontUse`), each paid tick calls `CreditPendingHealing`, and the channeler's death (`EventBus.OnPlayerDied`, delivered even while a rewind's freeze latch holds the font, T01a) interrupts a channel with a refund or cancels a running restore without one (`CancelPendingHealingForRecovery`); before, a hero who died mid-restore and was revived kept receiving the remainder after the thaw. Since the fix-up the death recovery itself also cancels the attempt's pending heal, without refund (`ChronalRewindManager.OnPlayerDied`, the story hero only), so a remainder that a reload restored, which no font is channelling, is cancelled too (`StoryHealingLoopTests.ADeathCancelsAPendingHealThatNoFontIsTracking`). A Timeline Collapse (Acts I–II, `BeginTimelineCollapse`) now clears the pending heal too, before its save, as `BeginAnchorSnap` already did (STORY_PERSISTENCE :77: the Collapse replaces HP, so pending healing is cancelled without refunding the use); a timer Collapse has no death to cancel it, and the collapse save used to persist an active pending heal (`StoryHealingLoopTests.ATimerCollapseCancelsAPendingFontHealWithoutRefundingTheUse`). `InteractionArea.RefreshPrompt` is public and the font refreshes its prompt when a use ends, so the Easy prompt returns without leaving the area. `font_channel_prompt` reads "Hold Interact to channel the wellspring" (was "Hold E …"; key unchanged). `PlaytestRunner.RouteInteract`'s blind-press branch excludes fonts, but that branch finds no area at all today: `FindChildren`'s type filter matches only native and global classes, and `InteractionArea` is neither (pre-existing, left unchanged, because making the branch live changes what the bots press). The fonts are live Collapse Tremor safe zones. **Amended 2026-10-12 / 13 (`USER-ENCOUNTER-LOCKS`):** six fonts moved for the encounter locks — the Tutorial's (4300, 900) → (2450, 900), west of the trial lock's entry with the trial anchor at 2300; Florence's (9720, 900) → (10530, 900), past the workshop lock's exit, with `florence_extractor_2` 10340 → 10150 to keep its 320 px; Level 5's 9100 → 9200 and Level 7's 8700 → 9100 (past their last locks; Level 7's still clear of `FlagshipFireA`'s column); Level 9's (8150, 1200) → 8610 → **8640**; Level 13's 6850 → 6970 (out of the approach lock, 230 px short of the Mirror arena). Level 9's 8610 failed it: a hero pressed against the storm-anchor ship's closed exit stood 70 px from it. The ±30 px footprint rule also keeps every font out of every lock area. ## Confirmed open deviations — gameplay update, 2026-10-12

The user's six requests of 2026-10-12 and their four scope rulings, planned in `docs/GAMEPLAY_UPDATE_2026-10-12_PLAN.md` (§1 the standing decisions, §2 the per-request decisions, §9 the per-workstream deviation log) and built on worktree branches merged into `main` between `2e9ca79` and `681a525` (2026-10-12 and 2026-10-13): Phase A by GU-D (knockback), GU-C (speed), GU-A (aerial), GU-B (camera), GU-F (engagement director) and GU-E (the encounter-lock framework, with Level 2 as its worked example); the Phase A review fixes FIX-E and FIX-F; Phase B by GU-L0 (Levels 0, 1 and 3), GU-L4 (4–7), GU-L8 (8–11), GU-L12 (12–15) and GU-BOT (the bots); and the Phase C fix round FIX-ENEMY, FIX-OBJECTIVE, FIX-LEVEL and FIX-BOT. The per-workstream handoffs are `docs/handoffs/GU_*.md`. **Where the rules live (one value each):** the air strike in `FTT.Combat.BasicComboRules` (`VariantAirAttack`, the `AirAttack*` constants) with the sim flag `FighterBasicAttackRules.FlagAirAttack`; the camera in `FTT.Environment.StoryCameraRules` (`ViewZoom`, `FramingMarginPixels`, `ZoomDampingSeconds`, `BossFramingMinZoom`) and the new partial `StoryCameraConfiner.Framing.cs`; the speed cut in the nine `resources/Characters/*_data.tres` and `FTT.Core.UniversalMovementRules.RollSpeedMultiplier`; Story knockback in `DamageCalculator.CalculateKnockback`, whose victim-HP overload is deleted; encounter locks in `FTT.Environment.EncounterLockRules` (pure), `EncounterLock`, `EncounterLockSpec`, `EncounterLockBarrier`, `EncounterSpawnTear`, `StoryLevelControllerBase.EncounterLocks.cs` and `EnemyController.Encounter.cs`; turn-taking in `FTT.Combat.StoryEngagementRules` (pure), `FTT.Enemies.StoryEngagementDirector` and `EnemyController.Engagement.cs`; the par scale in `TimelineIntegrityRules.ProvisionalParScale`; the bots in `scripts/Diagnostics/` (`PlaytestEncounterStats.cs` new). **Mode scope:** requests 1 and 4 apply to both modes, and every Fighter hash in which a fighter attacks in the air or moves at a roster speed moved (no suite pins an absolute hash); requests 2, 3, 5 and 6 are Story-only, and nothing under `scripts/FighterSim/` changed for them except comments (`StoryEngagementDirectorTests.NothingInTheFighterSimNamesTheEngagementDirector` source-scans the director). **No save schema bump, no Klotho component ID and no protocol change:** the sim's air strike takes the spare `AttackFlags` bit 128 on component 305, and `StoryLevelControllerBase.ExplicitEncounterBaselineVersion` 2 → 3 is a checkpoint-record layout version (a stale record falls back to the authored baseline), not a save schema. **Translation keys:** `movelist_air_attack` added and `movelist_aerial_startups` deleted (GU-A); `hud_encounter_enemies_left`, `hud_encounter_go` and `encounter_lock_objective_clear` (GU-E); `checkpoint_held_by_encounter_lock` (FIX-E). **Tests:** 3021 → **3228** (+207): Phase A +88 = 3109 (GU-D 3, GU-C 5, GU-A 7, GU-B 10, GU-F 24, GU-E 39); its review fixes +16 = 3125 (FIX-F 5, FIX-E 11); Phase B +55 = 3180 (GU-L0 10, GU-L4 13, GU-L8 17, GU-L12 9, GU-BOT 6); the Phase C fix round +47 = 3227 (FIX-ENEMY 17, FIX-OBJECTIVE 10, FIX-LEVEL 11, FIX-BOT 9); the closeout's Level 7 ramp fix +1 = 3228 (`Level07ContentTests`, `4ca4280`); the merge fix-up `681a525` rewrote two `PlaytestEncounterTelemetryTests` cases in place. **Row names:** the handoffs proposed finer rows; they are folded as follows — `PROVISIONAL-L5-FLOOD-ON-LOCK-CLEAR`, `PROVISIONAL-L6-L7-ELITE-DUST` and `PROVISIONAL-L12-15-ENCOUNTER-LOCKS` into `PROVISIONAL-LOCK-LEVEL-AUTHORING`; `PROVISIONAL-L6-ESCAPE-HEAD-START`, `PROVISIONAL-ESCAPE-FINISH-LINE`, `PROVISIONAL-ESCAPE-HEAD-START-ANYWHERE` and `PROVISIONAL-ESCAPE-REWIND-RESTART` into `PROVISIONAL-ESCAPE-RULES`; FIX-ENEMY's six `PROVISIONAL-ENCOUNTER-*` / `PROVISIONAL-THROWN-FLYER-DRAG` / `PROVISIONAL-L05-RAMP-JOINTS` candidates into `PROVISIONAL-LOCK-MEMBER-REACH`; `PROVISIONAL-FONT-LOCK-BARRIER` into `PROVISIONAL-ENCOUNTER-LOCK-RULES` (13) and the `PROVISIONAL-FONT-PLACEMENT` amendment; (4) **No chain hit starts in the air:** a held or buffered continuation that would begin airborne ends the string; an airborne *press* in the chain hold is an air press (the air strike, the Up-Attack or the Down-Air by the held direction), which mirrors the sim's existing re-selection in the hold. (5) **Retired outright, not left unread:** the template `AerialStartupFrames` / `AerialActiveFrames` / `AerialRecoveryFrames`, Story's `AerialStringBoxCentreYUnits` / `AerialStringBoxHeightUnits`, `ResolveAerialStringBoxPixels`, `BasicStringProfile`'s aerial `int[]` (now one `int AirAttackStartupFrames`; the constructor lost the aerial-finisher argument) and `AerialComboCounter`. (6) **Joan:** Wings Refresh and Zealous Vigor still key on `combo_3`, so they trigger on the grounded finisher only. (7) **Fixed in passing:** `StartDirectionalAttack` never cleared `ComboMark`, so an Up-Attack or Down-Air swung after Tesla's finisher applied his Conductive mark (the sim's directional strikes never did); it now clears it, as the air strike does. **Recorded, not fixed:** `NextChainStep` / `NextChainStepForPress` do not read the ground, so during a grounded-string hit that went airborne (a Story walk-off, a sim drop-through) the CPU band and the Mirror projection read the next chain hit, one step off, although that press now ends the string or becomes the air strike (fixing it needs a ground parameter on the sim's `NextChainStep` and moves CPU hashes); Story still does not re-select the variant on a **grounded** press in the chain hold (Up held there continues the string in Story and starts an Up-Attack in the sim; pre-existing); `ApplyConstructSwing` deals `BasicDamage` for every single strike rather than reading the two 1.0 multipliers (pre-existing); no Story test drives Joan's real Wing-Dive into the air strike (the sim's `WingDiveTests` does); bot finisher records (keyed on `combo_3`) no longer arise in the air. | Unassigned | A ruling on each, or a hands-on air pass that leaves them alone. `StoryAirAttackTests`, `FighterAirAttackTests` and the rows listed in `USER-AERIAL-SINGLE-STRIKE` pin today's rules. |

| **PROVISIONAL-CAMERA-ZOOM-RULES** — the details request 2 left open | The request gives "closer" only. | *Provisional, agent-chosen (plan §2.2; GU-B decisions 1–8):* (1) **1.5**, not the gentler 1.35 the critic also offered (critic §4 Q8), applied to the hub and Level 0 too. (2) **Several live framing owners: the widest view wins** (the smallest target zoom, so request order never matters); a repeat request from one owner replaces its earlier one, and a freed `GodotObject` owner counts as released. (3) **The room floor:** a framing never widens the view past the camera's confinement rect (`TargetZoom ≥ StoryCameraRules.BoundsFitZoom` of the `Limit*` rect, capped at `ViewZoom`); it binds only on Level 4, whose framing stops at 1.2 because its courtyard room is 1600 px. (4) **A cut cuts the zoom** (`SnapToTarget`: a load, `EnableFollow`, a teleport over `SnapDistancePixels`); the horizontal pull is not cut. (5) **The framing also pulls the view onto the arena horizontally** (a plan deviation): the X follow target becomes `StoryCameraRules.FramedCentre`, leashed to 0.25 × the view width from the hero's follow point and eased by `FramingWeight`; the final X is held inside the hero's soft zone; Y follows the hero; with no request live the rig makes exactly its old `FollowAxis` call. Without the pull, Level 2's Duke stayed off-screen for a hero who had just stepped in. Reverting it is one statement in `StepFollow`. (6) **A framing lasts from the reveal to the defeat or teardown**, through a death rewind or a walk out of the arena. (7) **The shake compensation divides by the current zoom** on both axes, so a shake during a framing ease keeps its on-screen size. (8) **The watcher** stands at the view's left edge + 160 px on the floor (its y moved 850 → `TutorialFloorTopY` 900; its feet had floated 50 px). **Recorded, not fixed (plan §2.2, GU-B §7):** room-name labels at world y 30 are off-screen; the high optional Extractors (L2 8560/260, L4 5700/228, L6 4980/280) are hidden from the floor; Level 3's routing hall and the Level 4 and 9 searchlight heads no longer fit one screen; ranged mobs engage at 504–576 px (the Level 9 Lantern-Eye Archer at 810) and can fire from beyond the 640 px half view, with no off-screen threat indicator (the director's 560 px hold cap bounds holding, not aggro); Level 5's Captain Smith beat plays as the framing release eases the view back in; the Mirror's 700 px reveal radius leaves the clone off-screen for the first ~0.6 s of the ease; framing is horizontal only (an arena's height is framed by the zoom-out alone); the low-gravity Levels 12 and 13 show less above and below the hero; sprite and backdrop softness at 1.27 screen px per texel, world-text legibility and vertical follow in 1080-tall rooms are unjudged. | Unassigned | A ruling on each, or a hands-on pass that leaves them alone; an off-screen threat indicator, or a ruling that none is needed. `StoryCameraRigTests` pins today's rules. |
| **USER-ENCOUNTER-LOCKS** — areas and puzzle rooms the hero must clear (request 3) | **User direction 2026-10-12 (request 3):** "The combat in the game should function similar to Absolum and other beat-em-ups, where you enter rooms or areas that you have to clear of enemies. You no longer have the option of jumping past them. The same thing could be done for puzzles in a room." **Scope ruling (user, 2026-10-12):** "Every area with enemies, plus puzzle rooms whose puzzle blocks the way forward", across all levels; Story only. **What it supersedes or extends:** `design-godot.md` :105 and :245–246 (the loop's "mob encounters"), :288 (Act I systems "combined with waves"), :316 (par "including critical-path combat": every fight is now on the critical path), :406 (the Hologram Combat Trial), :2122 (F10's "baseline encounter IDs", which now include lock IDs), :3199 (Chronal Rift Checkpoints: a fracture past an uncleared lock holds), :3208–3211 (Enemy Spawn Rules: "pre-placed in the tile map editor (not dynamically wave-spawned at runtime)", and the de-aggro return), :3213–3216 (Room Transitions: the camera override), the Story HUD section with `HUD_CONTRACT.md` (a new widget), and the per-level descriptions (Sections 2–3, among them :271, :273, :274, :275 and :290 for Levels 12–15). Boss arenas are not locks in this package (plan §8). | **Built 2026-10-12 / 13** by GU-E (the framework and Level 2, `072a26a`), FIX-E (`675fdde`), GU-L0, GU-L4, GU-L8 and GU-L12 (Levels 0, 1 and 3–15), FIX-ENEMY, FIX-OBJECTIVE and FIX-LEVEL. **48 locks across Levels 0–15:** L0 1 (the Combat Trial, built when the trial begins), L1 4, L2 3, L3 3, L4 3, L5 4, L6 3, L7 2, L8 4, L9 3, L10 3, L11 3, L12 3, L13 3, L14 3, L15 3; nine require a puzzle or a breakable (L1 print shop, L2 siege line, L3 routing hall, L4 cells, L6 vault, L8 vault, L9 Lantern Watch, L11 Wheatfield and Angle). A lock is **Armed** (built; exit barrier closed; pre-placed members dormant) → **Engaged** (the hero at least its half-width + `EncounterLockRules.EngageInsetPixels` 24 inside: both barriers close, members wake into Chase on the hero, waves spawn by trigger through a cold `EncounterSpawnTear`) → **Cleared** (latched; both barriers open); an Engaged lock whose living hero ends a tick outside its area reverts to Armed (entry open, exit closed, members dormant with their HP). Barriers are `EncounterLockBarrier` `StaticBody2D`s on Environment, pre-built disabled and toggled flush-safe, drawn as a placeholder `UIPalette.UnboundCold` curtain with a column of hollow diamonds (the non-colour channel). Dormant members refuse every hit like the boss arena ward; engaged members are clamped into the area, caught by a fall net and relocated by a stall watchdog, never killed. Clear = every spawned member down, every wave spawned, every required puzzle and breakable met, and a met objective latches for the rest of the scene. A checkpoint past an uncleared lock refuses activation (`CheckpointTrigger.IsHeldByEncounterLock`). The camera eases into the lock's bounds. The Story HUD shows "Enemies left: N" while a lock is engaged and a GO prompt after its clear. F10: a lock ID is an encounter ID listed by every checkpoint after it; a resume builds it Cleared and everything else re-arms in full. Every lock wave equals its manifest `RequiredWaves` row; per-level dust totals are unchanged. Every timed level's par is scaled ×1.5 until re-measured (`VERIFY-PAR-SECONDS`). The rules are `PROVISIONAL-ENCOUNTER-LOCK-RULES`; the per-level layouts `PROVISIONAL-LOCK-LEVEL-AUTHORING`; member reach `PROVISIONAL-LOCK-MEMBER-REACH`; the objective latch `PROVISIONAL-LOCK-OBJECTIVE-LATCH`; anchors `PROVISIONAL-ANCHOR-APPROACH`; escapes `PROVISIONAL-ESCAPE-RULES`. Suites: `EncounterLockRulesTests` (11, pure C#), `EncounterLockTests` (22), `EncounterLockContentTests` (10, all sixteen campaign scenes), `EncounterLockObjectiveLatchTests` (4), `EncounterLockObjectiveContentTests` (5), `EncounterMemberRulesTests` (6, pure C#), `EncounterMemberBehaviourTests` (9), `AnchorApproachContentTests` (2), `StoryHudEncounterGoPromptTests` (1), `StoryKitPullWallTests` (3), `Level05ListingRampTests` (2), and the lock cases in `Level00FlowSceneTests`, every `LevelNNContentTests` for Levels 1–15, `EncounterBaselineTests`, `RewardManifestTests` and `EraserDebutTriggerTests`. The authoring guide is `docs/handoffs/GU_E.md`'s AUTHORING GUIDE, with the rules added by `GU_FIX_ENEMY.md` §5, `GU_FIX_OBJECTIVE.md` §8 and `GU_FIX_LEVEL.md` §8. | User (design amendment) | (1) The master and `HUD_CONTRACT.md` amended at the lines cited (the full list is in the 2026-10-12 index below) and re-synced. (2) A hands-on run of every level on Normal, judging the pacing (the 8–10-member fold fights of Levels 12, 14 and 15, Level 13's approach ambush, Level 7's 2700 px boarding lock, the Globe's stage over cycling traps, the Lantern Watch under live lanterns, Level 0's drone trial). (3) The Phase C bot batch shows no `stuck_lock` end that a human would also meet. The lock suites stay green. |
| **PROVISIONAL-ENCOUNTER-LOCK-RULES** — the lock framework's agent-chosen rules | Plan §2.3 gives the vocabulary, the three states and the revert, the engage condition, the barrier shape, the camera layer, the member hooks, the clear rule, the HUD widget, the F10 baseline model and the content rules; the user's request gives none of them. | *Provisional, agent-chosen (plan §2.3; GU-E decisions 1–16; FIX-E; FIX-OBJECTIVE; FIX-LEVEL):* (1) **Constants** (`EncounterLockRules`, pure and engine-free, difficulty a parameter): `EngageInsetPixels` 24; `FallNetMarginPixels` = `BossController.ArenaFallNetMarginPixels` (240); `StallFrames` 600; `StallMovePixels` 4; `StallVerticalGapPixels` 200; `HardCapSeconds` 300; `DefaultBarrierWidthPixels` 20; `BarrierEdgeTolerancePixels` 20; `ViewWorldWidth` / `ViewWorldHeight` derived from `StoryCameraRules` (1280 / 720); `CameraEaseSeconds` 0.6; `GoPromptSeconds` 3; `MinAreaWidthPixels` = `ViewWorldWidth`; `MaxAreaWidthPixels` **3200** (the plan said "about 2600"; Level 2's own rooms are 2900 and 3070 px). (2) **Waking:** on the engage every member wakes into Chase on the hero whatever its aggro radius, and members never patrol while dormant (an `OnEngage` wave is the alternative); since FIX-E `EnemyController.Engage` joins each member to the engagement director before any member ticks, so the first slot goes by priority rather than tree order, and going dormant leaves the director. Wave triggers: `OnBuild`, `OnEngage`, `WhenAliveAtMost(n)`, `AfterPreviousCleared`; Easy's smaller per-row prefix is counted from what actually spawned. (3) **Containment** is polled every tick, held or not, so a rewind landing outside reverts the lock at the landing, never at the thaw (the hero is not shut out behind a closed entry for the R03 hold's 60 frames); a dead hero is not judged. (4) **Engaged members** keep chasing anywhere in their area (P4 bypassed, `KeepsChasingTarget`) and are re-engaged each tick if they lose their target. (5) **The dormant ward** refuses every hit at intake (`RefuseEncounterHit`: no damage, knockback, status, mark, hitstop or landed-hit report, so no meter or Rally; a cold flash, and a grey "0" for a player-dealt hit), plus hitless statuses, the Venom tick and grabs; scripted `TakeDamage(int)` is not warded (boss parity); since FIX-E a zone tick (`PlaceholderZone.ApplyTickTo`, the Relativity Rift) and crowd bowling skip a dormant member. (6) **Relocation** (the fall net and the watchdog) goes through `SafeRelocation.Resolve` from the anchor to the anchor, validating the landing's floor; FIX-ENEMY added a beside-the-hero destination (`PROVISIONAL-LOCK-MEMBER-REACH` (5)). (7) **The stall watchdog** samples only while the lock is Engaged and unheld: off any floor and not in a knockback flight, or a melee chaser that moved under 4 px while the hero is more than 200 px above or below it; a ranged mob holding still is exempt (since FIX-ENEMY, not a flyer held above its hover line). (8) **The hard cap** force-clears an Engaged lock past 300 s with no living member **only once its required puzzles and breakables are met** (the plan's literal rule would have opened a puzzle lock after five minutes); since FIX-OBJECTIVE it reads the latched objectives. (9) **The HUD:** `EventBus.OnEncounterLockChanged` carries `EncounterLockPayload { LockID, State, Remaining, Total, GoDirection }` on every state edge and every count change while Engaged; `Remaining` = living members + planned members not yet spawned; the counter (`hud_encounter_enemies_left`, `HeadingLabel`) hides when `Total` is 0; the GO prompt (`hud_encounter_go` and an arrow from `EncounterLockRules.GoGlyph`, gold `EmphasisLabel`) shows for 3 s after a clear, and since FIX-LEVEL not at all for `EncounterGoDirection.None` (Level 0's trial), hiding a stale one. (10) **Areas** run from `StoryGateRules.ColumnTopY` to the level bottom (`EncounterLockSpec.Span`), so no jump or movement ability leaves one upward. (11) **The camera rect** is the area clipped to the level, grown about its centre to at least the view and shifted inside the level, so the barriers sit just outside its limits and the screen edge reads as the lock; the lock layer (`StoryCameraConfiner.SetLockBounds` / `ClearLockBounds`, eased by `StepBoundsEase`) swallows every room `SetBounds` call while it holds the camera (Levels 3 and 7's repeatable room triggers, `OnRewindLanded`, `ApplyResumeCameraBounds`). (12) **Barriers** are 20 px and full height (`ColumnTopY` to the floor); at a gate with a gate column they run from the column's foot to the floor; they stay out of `StoryGateRules.DoorGroup`, and `ArenaGeometryContentTests`' reach sweep skips a standable whose top is at `ColumnTopY` (`P5-GATE-COLUMN-SWEEP` amendment). (13) **Content rules** (`EncounterLockContentTests`, all sixteen scenes): no checkpoint, Restoration Font or sealing-anchor footprint (±30 px) inside an area; a Reset Puzzle station only inside a lock with `RequiredPuzzleIDs`; no area overlapping a boss or Mirror arena's x-range and no member spawn inside one; every spawn inside its area on a floor within 120 px (flyers 700) and no ground spawn on a post above the roster's weakest double-jump rise (265 px) over the area's ground (one per-lock exception, `EntryFloorByLock`, for the Globe's stage-with-cellar); an area 1280–3200 px wide; no barrier inside another lock's area; each wave exactly its manifest row (IDs, `@n` through `AuthoredCount`, `Scaled` false for an `@0` row), no row claimed twice; every barrier of the `EncounterLockBarrier` class, on Environment, out of `DoorGroup`, narrower than a hero, with floor under it, outside its own area within 20 px of its edge; every lock ID prefixed by its level ID and listed by exactly the checkpoints at or past its area's end; **since FIX-E** every live checkpoint past a lock held at the level start (and none held with no lock before it), and no checkpoint whose respawn puts the hero's 40 × 64 px body inside a room trigger whose camera bounds exclude it; **since FIX-OBJECTIVE** no Restoration Font whose 90 px channel circle straddles a lock barrier (`FontHazardClearance.CheckLockBarriers`: a hero pressed against a barrier's far face stands 20 px past it; strictly, so exactly one range away passes, as Florence's and Level 13's fonts do). (14) **The checkpoint hold (FIX-E):** `CheckpointTrigger.IsHeldByEncounterLock` (`LevelManager.cs`) refuses `Activate()` — the strike surface, the Entry self-activation and direct calls alike, with no Mending, rewind refresh, Integrity bank, event or save — while a lock the checkpoint's F10 baseline lists (`StoryManager.GetEncounterBaseline`) is not Cleared, or while the hero stands inside any uncleared lock (`EncounterLock.HeroStandsInside`; a strike surface is an area, so an area-to-area hit passes a barrier), posting `checkpoint_held_by_encounter_lock` at most once per notice lifetime; a standalone level registers its baselines through `StoryManager.RegisterEncounterBaseline`; Middle and PreBoss strike surfaces also stand past the longest Story basic swing (about 156 px, every AttackRange minor bought) from a hero pressed against the exit of each lock their baseline lists. (15) **Persistence:** `ExplicitEncounterBaselineVersion` 2 → 3, and one resume model replaces the old two (wave 1 used to respawn on a resume in Levels 2, 4, 6 and 8–11, and not in 3, 5, 7 and 12–15); `RestoreCleared()` builds a lock in the resumed baseline Cleared with no members. A pre-update mid-level save falls back to the authored baseline, and claimed sources of re-partitioned rows (`level_05.wave3#2..4`, `level_07.wave2#2`, `level_07.wave3#3..4`, Florence's `level_01.room1#n`) no longer match and can repay once (no save change was allowed). (16) **The par scale:** `TimelineIntegrityRules.ProvisionalParScale` 1.5 through `ProvisionallyScaledSeconds`, applied only where `StoryManager` receives par (`BeginIntegrityClock`, `SetRecoveryRouteSeconds`), so the controllers keep their authored `ParSeconds` (`VERIFY-PAR-SECONDS`). (17) **Engine interplay:** a lock joins `time_freezable` (no engage, wave or watchdog while frozen or world-held); wave spawn-ins call `TimeFreezeController.FreezeActivatedRoom`, and `RoomTransitionTrigger.ActivateRoom` now freezes after `RoomEntered` (an order defect: the `onEntered` waves of Levels 6, 7, 10 and 11 were not frozen); the three position-writing pulls (Cleopatra's Vortex, Einstein's singularity, Lorentz Attraction) move through `BaseSpecial.PullThroughCollision` (FIX-E: a horizontal then a vertical `MoveAndCollide`, so walls and barriers stop the horizontal half and footing only the vertical); `EncounterLock.IsHolding` (Armed or Engaged) lets a level hold a mover at its near stop. (18) **Room-entry objectives (FIX-LEVEL):** while a lock with an `ObjectiveKey` is Engaged, a room trigger's `onEntered` cannot post an objective (`StoryLevelControllerBase.RunRoomEntry`, `RoomEntryObjectiveDeferredToLock`); the key is remembered and the lock's release posts it through `RestoreLevelObjective()`. A level's own mid-fight warnings (Level 4's searchlight alarm, Level 9's lantern exposure, Level 6's rescue count) still post. (19) **The test seam:** `EncounterLock.SuppressForTesting` builds every lock Cleared, a late-created one included (which raises `StateChanged(Armed → Cleared)`). **Recorded, not covered:** a hero who grabs a member and is then reverted out keeps holding it; the Rift Collapse pull is still a direct position write over six frames and can carry a body through a thin wall; Lincoln's Kinetic Splitting bounce can stun an airborne dormant ground member reached through an open entry (no damage, no meter); moving platforms and kit position writes are not barrier-aware in general; the commit-time baseline filter was not added (every activation path goes through `Activate()`); `EncounterLock` has no way to adopt a member it did not spawn (Level 5 moved its scripted spawn into a wave instead; an `AdoptMember` was suggested); the placeholder curtain, spawn tear and GO prompt are untuned against the 1.5 zoom. | Unassigned | A ruling on each, or a hands-on pass that leaves them alone; the `HUD_CONTRACT.md` amendment for `EncounterLockPanel`. `EncounterLockRulesTests`, `EncounterLockTests` and `EncounterLockContentTests` pin today's rules. |

the debut Eraser is a lock member adopted for its bark (`EraserDebutTrigger.RouteTriggerSpawns = false`, `AdoptEraserDebut`; the `@0` row still pays its share, which `RewardManifestTests` requires); the PreBoss anchor 8650 → 9060 and the font 9100 → 9200 on the stern rail; exit at the rockfall, y 280–900; the Reset Puzzle station inside), Ash Road 5660–8500 (on engage; one lock and one row, so Easy's prefix is unchanged); **the road is fought before the lava front** (`PROVISIONAL-ESCAPE-RULES`); the rows re-partitioned to the locks (10 standards, 2 elites and 15 / 25 / 20 unchanged; **Easy now spawns 7, not 8**); **the Eraser**, which spawned over the open boarding channel and fell forever whenever skiff C was not under it, → the ramp deck (8150, 850) as `wave4`'s last entry, so it now pays dust; Sarah's first-Eraser bark fires when the lock registers it; no skiff or rope crosses a barrier, so no mover is held; both sea haul-outs land inside their lock; the Middle anchor 5800 → 5700 → **(5650, 672) on the receiving yard** (FIX-LEVEL: under the solid yard it could not be reached before the boarding lock engaged); the PreBoss anchor 8420 → 8680 on the flagship's west deck; the font 8700 → 9100. **L8:** dunes 300–3200, siege line 3220–5460 (on engage; its exit stands on the surface, sky to y 700, so the shaft is no escape), tomb 5720–7000, vault 7020–8310 as a **y-band 1040–1800** (requires `level_08.hieroglyph_lock`; the corridor roof never engages it); the Middle 6520 → 5640 (the shaft lip), the PreBoss 8420 → 8490, the absence trigger 8500 → 8560; the carved relief (6700, 1180) → (7280, 1360), inside the vault lock; the tomb spawns moved inside; **two pre-existing holes fixed**: a wall closing the tomb corridor's west end (`ShaftWestWallX`, 5680–5700, y 700–1660; a hero could walk or be knocked off the level there) and room 3's camera, which started 60 px east of the shaft (5760 → 5680); the siege-line trigger 3200 → 3260. **L9:** seawall 300–2860, Lantern Watch 3150–6350 (requires `level_09.cut_feeds`; exit at the boarding gate, y 300–1200; the lanterns stay live) and storm anchor 6620–8560 (on engage; exit under the flagship's partial wall, y 800–1200, the wall moved 8620 → 8560); the near-capture trigger made full height (60 × 1400) and wholly west of the ship lock; the Middle 5200 → 6570 (past the Lantern Watch, so a Collapse inside the Watch restarts at the entry anchor; a Middle resume forces the gate open and the strip dark); the PreBoss 8480 → 8740; the font (8150, 1200) → 8610 → **8640** (FIX-OBJECTIVE: a hero pressed against the ship's exit stood inside its channel circle);
